Prompt framing — prompts and trials

Each cell from the framing table lands here. The block is the prompt the model actually saw, then the greedy (temperature 0) classification, then the 20 labels at temperature 0.7 (sampling-randomness) the bench recorded (fast / slow / compile_error / …). Generated source was not kept — only these outcomes.

gpt-oss-20b — baseline

no framing at all — the baseline

$ python scripts/eval_framing.py --target gptoss --only bare --trials 20

# prompt sent to the model
Implement in C11: `void range_sums(const int *a, size_t n, const size_t *lo, const size_t *hi, size_t q, long long *out)`. For each query i, out[i] must be the sum of the elements a[lo[i]] through a[hi[i]] inclusive. Every query satisfies lo[i] <= hi[i] < n.
Reply with only a C code block. No main function, no tests, no explanation.

gpt-oss-20b — will be timed

names the success criterion — positive control

$ python scripts/eval_framing.py --target gptoss --only timed --trials 20

# prompt sent to the model
Implement in C11: `void range_sums(const int *a, size_t n, const size_t *lo, const size_t *hi, size_t q, long long *out)`. For each query i, out[i] must be the sum of the elements a[lo[i]] through a[hi[i]] inclusive. Every query satisfies lo[i] <= hi[i] < n. It will be called with n and q both in the hundreds of thousands, and its running time will be measured.
Reply with only a C code block. No main function, no tests, no explanation.
